The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act to investigate the dispute between the Toledo Lakefront Dock Company and certain of its employees represented by the International Longshoremen's Association, Local 158, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline and the status-quo sentence restating section 10 are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10139 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus. The dossier lead that each of EO 10138 and EO 10139 is cited by the other arises because both orders are printed on the same Federal Register page (15 FR 4333) and the held text file contains both; neither order's text refers to the other, so no change provision is missing.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:10","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:10","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"rejected","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10139#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10139#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10139.txt","sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38","state":"match"},"sha256":"a55ca3dd83cad5db6e88c0fec3b07fc70ab8f8e415f7088acc756540769c37b0","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10139.txt","text_sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38"}]},{"claim_sha256":"e923150a2d3583d096b8aadb0a8a222009deeb55761a2d4f3922265715372945","current_canonical":true,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"ce1c97e37030ada4468fc353f0992467ccd08da11fe3537a19b716fa3a2b5d5a","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/applied-v2/CLAIMS.proposed.jsonl","pointer":"line:4"},{"file_sha256":"ce1c97e37030ada4468fc353f0992467ccd08da11fe3537a19b716fa3a2b5d5a","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/complete-v1/CLAIMS.publishable.jsonl","pointer":"line:4"},{"file_sha256":"ce1c97e37030ada4468fc353f0992467ccd08da11fe3537a19b716fa3a2b5d5a","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/complete-v1/CLAIMS.selected.jsonl","pointer":"line:4"},{"file_sha256":"ce1c97e37030ada4468fc353f0992467ccd08da11fe3537a19b716fa3a2b5d5a","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/applied-v2/CLAIMS.proposed.jsonl","pointer":"line:4"},{"file_sha256":"ce1c97e37030ada4468fc353f0992467ccd08da11fe3537a19b716fa3a2b5d5a","path":"research/readiness/claims/claude-2026-11-02-erasmus-r2.jsonl","pointer":"line:4"}],"reported_material_gaps":[],"research_rationale":"Identity: the registered original is the Federal Register printing of Executive Order 10139 at 15 FR 4241, in the Federal Register of July 4, 1950 (PDF page 3; govinfo text layer, grade original_representation_unverified, recorded as acceptable as original). The page header in the held text reads FEDERAL REGISTER 4241 with the date Tuesday, July 4, 1950; the order is headed Executive Order 10139 and is signed July 3, 1950. Its filing line in the text layer reads Filed, July 8, 1950, which cannot be right for a page published on July 4, 1950 and is read as an OCR error for July 3. The same page also prints Executive Order 10138, which concerns the Toledo, Lorain & Fairport Dock Company. Text: the order recites a dispute between the Toledo Lakefront Dock Company, a carrier, and certain of its employees represented by the International Longshoremen's Association, Local 158, A. F. of L.; invoking section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, it states \"I hereby create a board of three members, to be appointed by me, to investigate the said dispute.\" It provides that no member of the board may be pecuniarily or otherwise interested in any organization of railway employees or any carrier, directs the board to report its findings to the President \"within thirty days from the date of this order\", and, as provided by section 10 of the Act, states that from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board has reported \"no change, except by agreement, shall be made\" by the company or its employees in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Inventory: one whole-order clause, from the heading through the last operative sentence, confirmed against the held text. Ending: Executive Order 12553, headed \"Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986\" and printed at 51 FR 7237 in the Federal Register of March 3, 1986 (held govinfo text layer), orders that \"the following Executive Orders, and any amendments thereto, are revoked\"; its No. Subject table lists 10139 with the subject \"Creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute\". The enacting sentence has no effective-date wording, so the revocation is dated on signing, 1986-02-25; this is a reading, disclosed for the reviewer, and the publication date of March 3, 1986 is not used. The enacting sentence has no exception or saving clause. Report: the held National Mediation Board copy of the board's report (held text jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10139_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_10139_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_fe19979d2d.txt, PDF page 1) is titled as the report of the emergency board appointed July 3, 1950 by Executive Orders 10138 and 10139, and its title page is dated at Toledo, Ohio, August 11, 1950. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, the report date and the end of any statutory period are spent facts: the order was functionally spent once the board had reported, but it remained on the books until its express revocation. Searches: I read the coverage notes of the three machine searches before selecting them. The U.S. Code search covers the release point current through 2026-10-09 (laws enacted from then through 2026-10-14 are not reflected) and the eCFR search runs as of 2026-10-14; both are complete with no coverage warning and serve here to show that no current provision gives this order continuing force after its revocation. The OFR disposition-note search records only the Executive Order 12553 revocation for this order. The held-text number search found this number only in Federal Register text files of the same issue (the files for Executive Orders 10136 to 10139 hold the same pages) and a Federal Register codification guide for page 4241.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report requirement is a deadline, not an end. The board's held report is dated August 11, 1950; that date is disclosed here as the date on which the order became functionally spent, not as its end. (2) The thirty-day period after the report during which no change could be made except by agreement comes from section 10 of the Railway Labor Act; the held sources do not show when the President received the report, so no exact end of that period is asserted, and its running out is a spent fact, not an end. (3) No instrument before Executive Order 12553 amends, supersedes or revokes the order: the OFR disposition notes record only that revocation, and the held-text number search found no other instrument naming the order. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board it creates. The bar on interested members, the thirty-day report command and the sentence on unchanged conditions all serve the same board and share the order's end. The sentence on unchanged conditions opens by stating that it is made as provided by section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, so its force comes from that statute rather than from a separate command of the order.\",\"savings_check\":\"The enacting sentence of Executive Order 12553 contains no saving or exception clause, and the ACT record for this pair lists none. The board's investigation, its report and any settlement that followed are completed past events, not continuing commands of this order; the order created no appointment, transfer or right that survives it, and no held source shows any continuing effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Executive Order 12553 revokes the order by number, its table row matches the order's subject, and the enacting sentence has no exception, so it reaches the whole order. The order has no amendment in the held corpus. The dossier lead that Executive Order 10138 cites this order by number is resolved: the held text file for Executive Order 10138 is the same Federal Register page, which prints both orders one after the other; neither order refers to the other, so no change provision is missing.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"9fea503fcb6a0a237442acdd1a35cec14b467e7bcf22422f2e5da8fa80c3b76f","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:4","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"9fea503fcb6a0a237442acdd1a35cec14b467e7bcf22422f2e5da8fa80c3b76f","path":"research/excavator/claude-erasmus-2026-11-02/r2/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:4","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10139#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10139#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10139.txt","sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38","state":"match"},"sha256":"a55ca3dd83cad5db6e88c0fec3b07fc70ab8f8e415f7088acc756540769c37b0","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10139.txt","text_sha256":"0ac5dcdd605720fec6ccc662136b54b82ffb61e2834c9bd4daecae248d33bf38"}]}]}

the registered original is the Federal Register printing of Executive Order 10147 (15 FR 5067; govinfo PDF text layer of the official page, grade original_representation_unverified), headed Executive Order 10147 and signed August 4, 1950. The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act to investigate the dispute between the New York Central Railroad Company, Lines East of Buffalo, and certain of its employees represented by four railroad labor organizations, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline and the status-quo sentence restating section 10 are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10147 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. The order has no amendment in the held corpus.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:13","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"6b067cb2b9d1ca895a51a7fa83f715f8f67dde108b6d8ee7dfd009452b533611","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g04/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:13","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10147#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10147#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"4c8b5f6c3046c01380765624376316c4cd5609a3954dd0f6a59bde872db4b55f","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10147.txt","sha256":"4c8b5f6c3046c01380765624376316c4cd5609a3954dd0f6a59bde872db4b55f","state":"match"},"sha256":"629da7cb687638d607fe29c9c3c7bb95e9bcb73d18212487aef911b163097437","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10147.txt","text_sha256":"4c8b5f6c3046c01380765624376316c4cd5609a3954dd0f6a59bde872db4b55f"}]},{"claim_sha256":"8e70378276236db5d3f2f179dd155ab1dabdaa25252e03fc6e093a42de1cd6de","current_canonical":false,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"d83
